DEFINE FAST list_set lid2: (lid2) i: (i) val: (val):
  set list_set:sz to (item (lid2) of l_count)
  set list_set:ok to 1
  set list_set:i2 to (i)
  IF list_set:i2 < 0 THEN:
    set list_set:i2 to (list_set:sz + list_set:i2)
  IF (list_set:i2 < 0) or (not (list_set:i2 < list_set:sz)) THEN:
    set err to 1
    set errmsg to "IndexError: list assignment index out of range"
    set list_set:ok to 0
  IF list_set:ok = 1 THEN:
    replace item ((item (lid2) of l_start) + list_set:i2) of l_items with (val)

DEFINE FAST nd_add nid2: (nid2) val: (val):
  add (val) to nd_items
  replace item (nid2) of nd_count with ((item (nid2) of nd_count) + 1)
  change ni by 1

DEFINE FAST write_str s: (s):
  set write_str:slen to (length of (s))
  set write_str:si to 1
  REPEAT write_str:slen:
    set write_str:c to (letter write_str:si of (s))
    IF ("z" join write_str:c) = "z
" THEN:
      add cur_line to response
      set cur_line to ""
    ELSE:
      set cur_line to (cur_line join write_str:c)
    change write_str:si by 1

DEFINE FAST lexer_init code: (code):
  set src to (code)
  set pos to 1
  set srclen to (length of src)
  set line to 1
  set col to 1
  set ch to ""
  set at_line_start to 1
  set paren_depth to 0
  set has_pending to 0
  set pending_dedent_count to 0
  delete all of indent_stack
  add 0 to indent_stack
  set _last_toks to ""
  IF srclen > 0 THEN:
    set ch to (letter 1 of src)

DEFINE FAST list_slice_assign lid2: (lid2) start: (start) stop: (stop) step: (step) rhs: (rhs):
  set list_slice_assign:sz to (item (lid2) of l_count)
  set list_slice_assign:st to (start)
  set list_slice_assign:en to (stop)
  set list_slice_assign:sp to (step)
  vtype v: (rhs)
  set @34 to vtype:return
  set list_slice_assign:rhs_t to @34
  set list_slice_assign:rhs_lid to 0
  set list_slice_assign:ok to 1
  IF (("z" join list_slice_assign:rhs_t) = "zl") or (("z" join list_slice_assign:rhs_t) = "zt") THEN:
    vid v: (rhs)
    set @35 to vid:return
    set list_slice_assign:rhs_lid to @35
  ELSE:
    set err to 1
    set errmsg to "TypeError: can only assign an iterable"
    set list_slice_assign:ok to 0
  IF list_slice_assign:ok = 1 THEN:
    IF list_slice_assign:sp = 1 THEN:
      IF list_slice_assign:st < -9999999 THEN:
        set list_slice_assign:st to 0
      IF list_slice_assign:en < -9999999 THEN:
        set list_slice_assign:en to list_slice_assign:sz
      IF list_slice_assign:st < 0 THEN:
        set list_slice_assign:st to (list_slice_assign:sz + list_slice_assign:st)
      IF list_slice_assign:en < 0 THEN:
        set list_slice_assign:en to (list_slice_assign:sz + list_slice_assign:en)
      IF list_slice_assign:st < 0 THEN:
        set list_slice_assign:st to 0
      IF list_slice_assign:en > list_slice_assign:sz THEN:
        set list_slice_assign:en to list_slice_assign:sz
      set list_slice_assign:ns to li
      set list_slice_assign:j to 0
      REPEAT list_slice_assign:sz:
        IF (list_slice_assign:j < list_slice_assign:st) or (not (list_slice_assign:j < list_slice_assign:en)) THEN:
          list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_slice_assign:j
          set @36 to list_get:return
          add @36 to l_items
          change li by 1
        IF list_slice_assign:j = list_slice_assign:st THEN:
          set list_slice_assign:k to 0
          REPEAT (item list_slice_assign:rhs_lid of l_count):
            list_get lid2: list_slice_assign:rhs_lid i: list_slice_assign:k
            set @37 to list_get:return
            add @37 to l_items
            change li by 1
            change list_slice_assign:k by 1
        change list_slice_assign:j by 1
      replace item (lid2) of l_start with list_slice_assign:ns
      replace item (lid2) of l_count with ((list_slice_assign:st + (item list_slice_assign:rhs_lid of l_count)) + (list_slice_assign:sz - list_slice_assign:en))
    ELSE:
      set err to 1
      set errmsg to "NotImplementedError: extended slice assignment with step != 1 not fully supported"

DEFINE FAST dict_set did2: (did2) key: (key) val: (val):
  set dict_set:start to (item (did2) of d_start)
  set dict_set:cnt to (item (did2) of d_count)
  set dict_set:found to 0
  set dict_set:i to 0
  REPEAT UNTIL (dict_set:found = 1) or (not (dict_set:i < dict_set:cnt)):
    set dict_set:idx to (dict_set:start + dict_set:i)
    str_eq s1: (item dict_set:idx of d_keys) s2: (key)
    set @38 to str_eq:return
    IF @38 = 1 THEN:
      replace item dict_set:idx of d_vals with (val)
      set dict_set:found to 1
    IF not (dict_set:found = 1) THEN:
      change dict_set:i by 1
  IF not (dict_set:found = 1) THEN:
    IF dict_set:cnt = 0 THEN:
      replace item (did2) of d_start with d_idx
    ELSE:
      set dict_set:expected_next to ((item (did2) of d_start) + (item (did2) of d_count))
      IF not (dict_set:expected_next = d_idx) THEN:
        set dict_set:new_start to d_idx
        set dict_set:j to 0
        REPEAT (item (did2) of d_count):
          add (item ((item (did2) of d_start) + dict_set:j) of d_keys) to d_keys
          add (item ((item (did2) of d_start) + dict_set:j) of d_vals) to d_vals
          change d_idx by 1
          change dict_set:j by 1
        replace item (did2) of d_start with dict_set:new_start
    add (key) to d_keys
    add (val) to d_vals
    replace item (did2) of d_count with ((item (did2) of d_count) + 1)
    change d_idx by 1

DEFINE FAST list_insert lid2: (lid2) i: (i) val: (val):
  set list_insert:sz to (item (lid2) of l_count)
  set list_insert:ii to (i)
  IF list_insert:ii < 0 THEN:
    set list_insert:ii to (list_insert:sz + list_insert:ii)
  IF list_insert:ii < 0 THEN:
    set list_insert:ii to 0
  IF list_insert:ii > list_insert:sz THEN:
    set list_insert:ii to list_insert:sz
  set list_insert:ns to li
  set list_insert:j to 0
  REPEAT list_insert:sz:
    IF list_insert:j = list_insert:ii THEN:
      add (val) to l_items
      change li by 1
    list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_insert:j
    set @39 to list_get:return
    set list_insert:item to @39
    add list_insert:item to l_items
    change li by 1
    change list_insert:j by 1
  IF list_insert:ii = list_insert:sz THEN:
    add (val) to l_items
    change li by 1
  replace item (lid2) of l_start with list_insert:ns
  replace item (lid2) of l_count with (list_insert:sz + 1)

DEFINE FAST list_sort lid2: (lid2):
  set list_sort:sz to (item (lid2) of l_count)
  IF list_sort:sz > 1 THEN:
    set list_sort:i to 1
    REPEAT UNTIL not (list_sort:i < list_sort:sz):
      set list_sort:j to list_sort:i
      REPEAT UNTIL not (list_sort:j > 0):
        IF list_sort:j > 0 THEN:
          set list_sort:prev to (list_sort:j - 1)
          list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_sort:j
          set @2646 to list_get:return
          list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_sort:prev
          set @2647 to list_get:return
          vlt a: @2646 b: @2647
          set @40 to vlt:return
          IF @40 = 1 THEN:
            list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_sort:j
            set @41 to list_get:return
            set list_sort:tmp to @41
            list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_sort:prev
            set @42 to list_get:return
            list_set lid2: (lid2) i: list_sort:j val: @42
            list_set lid2: (lid2) i: list_sort:prev val: list_sort:tmp
          ELSE:
            set list_sort:j to 0
        change list_sort:j by -1
      change list_sort:i by 1

DEFINE FAST list_reverse lid2: (lid2):
  set list_reverse:sz to (item (lid2) of l_count)
  set list_reverse:i to 0
  REPEAT (floor of (list_reverse:sz / 2)):
    set list_reverse:j to ((list_reverse:sz - 1) - list_reverse:i)
    list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_reverse:i
    set @43 to list_get:return
    set list_reverse:tmp to @43
    list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_reverse:j
    set @44 to list_get:return
    list_set lid2: (lid2) i: list_reverse:i val: @44
    list_set lid2: (lid2) i: list_reverse:j val: list_reverse:tmp
    change list_reverse:i by 1

DEFINE FAST list_append lid2: (lid2) val: (val):
  IF (item (lid2) of l_count) = 0 THEN:
    replace item (lid2) of l_start with li
  ELSE:
    set list_append:expected_next to ((item (lid2) of l_start) + (item (lid2) of l_count))
    IF not (list_append:expected_next = li) THEN:
      set list_append:new_start to li
      set list_append:j to 0
      REPEAT (item (lid2) of l_count):
        add (item ((item (lid2) of l_start) + list_append:j) of l_items) to l_items
        change li by 1
        change list_append:j by 1
      replace item (lid2) of l_start with list_append:new_start
  add (val) to l_items
  replace item (lid2) of l_count with ((item (lid2) of l_count) + 1)
  change li by 1

    set dict_update:cnt to (item dict_update:oid of d_count)
    set dict_update:i to 0
    REPEAT dict_update:cnt:
      dict_set did2: (did2) key: (item (dict_update:start + dict_update:i) of d_keys) val: (item (dict_update:start + dict_update:i) of d_vals)
      change dict_update:i by 1

DEFINE FAST parse_req_single rs: (rs):
  delete all of request
  delete all of input_data
  set parse_req_single:rslen to (length of (rs))
  set parse_req_single:seg to ""
  set parse_req_single:i to 1
  set parse_req_single:seg_num to 0
  set parse_req_single:parse_done to 0
  REPEAT parse_req_single:rslen:
    IF not (parse_req_single:parse_done = 1) THEN:
      set parse_req_single:c to (letter parse_req_single:i of (rs))
      set parse_req_single:matched to 0
      IF not ((parse_req_single:i + 2) > parse_req_single:rslen) THEN:
        IF ((("z" join (letter parse_req_single:i of (rs))) = "z§") and (("z" join (letter (parse_req_single:i + 1) of (rs))) = "z§")) and (("z" join (letter (parse_req_single:i + 2) of (rs))) = "z§") THEN:
          set parse_req_single:matched to 1
      IF parse_req_single:matched = 1 THEN:
        set err to 0
        unescape_str s: parse_req_single:seg
        set @269 to unescape_str:return
        set parse_req_single:decoded to @269
        IF err = 1 THEN:
          set parse_req_single:parse_done to 1
        ELSE:
          IF parse_req_single:seg_num = 0 THEN:
            add parse_req_single:decoded to request
          ELSE:
            add parse_req_single:decoded to input_data
          change parse_req_single:seg_num by 1
          set parse_req_single:seg to ""
          change parse_req_single:i by 2
      ELSE:
        set parse_req_single:seg to (parse_req_single:seg join parse_req_single:c)
    change parse_req_single:i by 1
  IF not (parse_req_single:parse_done = 1) THEN:
    set err to 0
    unescape_str s: parse_req_single:seg
    set @270 to unescape_str:return
    set parse_req_single:decoded to @270
    IF not (err = 1) THEN:
      IF parse_req_single:seg_num = 0 THEN:
        add parse_req_single:decoded to request
      ELSE:
        add parse_req_single:decoded to input_data

  set @3830 to vnum:return
  vnum v: (b)
  set @3990 to vnum:return
  m_pow2 b: (floor of @3990)
  set @3831 to m_pow2:return
  mk_num n: ((floor of @3830) * @3831)
  set @2681 to mk_num:return
  set bitwise_shl:return to @2681

DEFINE FAST parse_shift:
  parse_arith
  set @318 to parse_arith:return
  set parse_shift:l to @318
  IF err = 1 THEN:
    mk_num n: 0
    set @2682 to mk_num:return
    set parse_shift:return to @2682
    stop this script
  set parse_shift:done to 0
  REPEAT UNTIL parse_shift:done = 1:
    IF tok = "<<" THEN:
      next_tok
      set @319 to next_tok:return
      set _d to @319
      IF err = 1 THEN:
        mk_num n: 0
        set @2683 to mk_num:return
        set parse_shift:return to @2683
        stop this script
      _push v: parse_shift:l
      _push v: parse_shift:done
      parse_arith
      set @320 to parse_arith:return
      set parse_shift:r to @320
      _pop
      set @321 to _pop:return
      set parse_shift:done to @321
      _pop
      set @322 to _pop:return
      set parse_shift:l to @322
      IF err = 1 THEN:
        mk_num n: 0
        set @2684 to mk_num:return
        set parse_shift:return to @2684
        stop this script
      mk_str s: "<<"
      set @2685 to mk_str:return
      mk_node2 nt: "args" a: parse_shift:l b: parse_shift:r
      set @2686 to mk_node2:return
      mk_node2 nt: "binop" a: @2685 b: @2686
      set @323 to mk_node2:return
      set parse_shift:l to @323
    ELSE:
      IF tok = ">>" THEN:
        next_tok
        set @324 to next_tok:return
        set _d to @324
        IF err = 1 THEN:
          mk_num n: 0
          set @2687 to mk_num:return
          set parse_shift:return to @2687
          stop this script
        _push v: parse_shift:l
        _push v: parse_shift:done
        parse_arith
        set @325 to parse_arith:return
        set parse_shift:r to @325
        _pop
        set @326 to _pop:return
        set parse_shift:done to @326
        _pop
        set @327 to _pop:return
        set parse_shift:l to @327
        IF err = 1 THEN:
          mk_num n: 0
          set @2688 to mk_num:return
          set parse_shift:return to @2688
          stop this script
        mk_str s: ">>"
        set @2689 to mk_str:return
        mk_node2 nt: "args" a: parse_shift:l b: parse_shift:r
        set @2690 to mk_node2:return
        mk_node2 nt: "binop" a: @2689 b: @2690
        set @328 to mk_node2:return
        set parse_shift:l to @328
      ELSE:
        set parse_shift:done to 1
  set parse_shift:return to parse_shift:l

DEFINE FAST s_startswith s: (s) pre: (pre):
  vstr v: (s)
  set @329 to vstr:return
  set s_startswith:ss to @329
  vstr v: (pre)
  set @330 to vstr:return
  set s_startswith:pp to @330
  set s_startswith:pl to (length of s_startswith:pp)
  IF s_startswith:pl > (length of s_startswith:ss) THEN:
    set s_startswith:return to 0
    stop this script
  s_sub s: s_startswith:ss start: 1 end: (s_startswith:pl + 1)
  set @3832 to s_sub:return
  str_eq s1: @3832 s2: s_startswith:pp
  set @2691 to str_eq:return
  set s_startswith:return to @2691

DEFINE FAST _pop:
  set _pop:sp to (length of _save_stack)
  set _pop:val to (item _pop:sp of _save_stack)
  delete _pop:sp of _save_stack
  set _pop:return to _pop:val

DEFINE FAST py_in container: (container) item: (item):
  vtype v: (container)
  set @442 to vtype:return
  set py_in:t to @442
  vtype v: (item)
  set @443 to vtype:return
  IF (("z" join py_in:t) = "zs") and (("z" join @443) = "zs") THEN:
    vstr v: (container)
    set @444 to vstr:return
    set py_in:hay to @444
    vstr v: (item)
    set @445 to vstr:return
    set py_in:needle to @445
    set py_in:hl to (length of py_in:hay)
    set py_in:nl to (length of py_in:needle)
    IF py_in:nl = 0 THEN:
      set py_in:return to 1
      stop this script
    IF py_in:nl > py_in:hl THEN:
      set py_in:return to 0
      stop this script
    set py_in:i to 1
    REPEAT UNTIL py_in:i > ((py_in:hl - py_in:nl) + 1):
      set py_in:m to 1
      set py_in:j to 0
      REPEAT py_in:nl:
        str_eq s1: (letter (py_in:i + py_in:j) of py_in:hay) s2: (letter (py_in:j + 1) of py_in:needle)
        set @446 to str_eq:return
        IF not (@446 = 1) THEN:
          set py_in:m to 0
        change py_in:j by 1
      IF py_in:m = 1 THEN:
        set py_in:return to 1
        stop this script
      change py_in:i by 1
    set py_in:return to 0
    stop this script
  IF (("z" join py_in:t) = "zl") or (("z" join py_in:t) = "zt") THEN:
    vid v: (container)
    set @447 to vid:return
    set py_in:lid2 to @447
    set py_in:i to 0
    REPEAT (item py_in:lid2 of l_count):
      list_get lid2: py_in:lid2 i: py_in:i
      set @2777 to list_get:return
      veq a: @2777 b: (item)
      set @448 to veq:return
      IF @448 = 1 THEN:
        set py_in:return to 1
        stop this script
      change py_in:i by 1
    set py_in:return to 0
    stop this script
  IF ("z" join py_in:t) = "zd" THEN:
    vid v: (container)
    set @3868 to vid:return
    dkey v: (item)
    set @3869 to dkey:return
    dict_has did2: @3868 key: @3869
    set @2778 to dict_has:return
    set py_in:return to @2778
    stop this script
  IF ("z" join py_in:t) = "zi" THEN:
    inst_id v: (container)
    set @449 to inst_id:return
    set py_in:did2 to (item @449 of i_dict)
    dkey v: (item)
    set @3870 to dkey:return
    dict_has_inherited did2: py_in:did2 key: @3870
    set @2779 to dict_has_inherited:return
    set py_in:return to @2779
    stop this script
  set py_in:return to 0

DEFINE FAST bitwise_and a: (a) b: (b):
  vnum v: (a)
  set @455 to vnum:return
  set bitwise_and:x to (floor of @455)
  vnum v: (b)
  set @456 to vnum:return
  set bitwise_and:y to (floor of @456)
  set bitwise_and:r to 0
  set bitwise_and:bit to 1
  REPEAT UNTIL (bitwise_and:x = 0) and (bitwise_and:y = 0):
    IF ((bitwise_and:x mod 2) = 1) and ((bitwise_and:y mod 2) = 1) THEN:
      change bitwise_and:r by bitwise_and:bit
    set bitwise_and:x to (floor of (bitwise_and:x / 2))
    set bitwise_and:y to (floor of (bitwise_and:y / 2))
    set bitwise_and:bit to (bitwise_and:bit * 2)
  mk_num n: bitwise_and:r
  set @2785 to mk_num:return
  set bitwise_and:return to @2785

DEFINE FAST unescape_str s: (s):
  set unescape_str:slen to (length of (s))
  set unescape_str:si to 1
  set unescape_str:seg to ""
  REPEAT unescape_str:slen:
    set unescape_str:c to (letter unescape_str:si of (s))
    IF ("z" join unescape_str:c) = "z\" THEN:
      change unescape_str:si by 1
      IF unescape_str:si > unescape_str:slen THEN:
        set err to 1
        set errmsg to "ValueError: Request single format error: trailing backslash"
        set unescape_str:return to ""
        stop this script
      set unescape_str:ec to (letter unescape_str:si of (s))
      ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: "n"
      set @457 to ceq:return
      IF @457 = 1 THEN:
        set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join "
")
      ELSE:
        ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: "t"
        set @458 to ceq:return
        IF @458 = 1 THEN:
          set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join "	")
        ELSE:
          ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: "r"
          set @459 to ceq:return
          IF @459 = 1 THEN:
            set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join "")
          ELSE:
            ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: "\"
            set @460 to ceq:return
            IF @460 = 1 THEN:
              set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join "\")
            ELSE:
              ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: "§"
              set @461 to ceq:return
              IF @461 = 1 THEN:
                set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join "§")
              ELSE:
                ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: "'"
                set @462 to ceq:return
                IF @462 = 1 THEN:
                  set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join "'")
                ELSE:
                  ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: """
                  set @463 to ceq:return
                  IF @463 = 1 THEN:
                    set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join """)
                  ELSE:
                    ceq a: unescape_str:ec b: "0"
                    set @464 to ceq:return
                    IF @464 = 1 THEN:
                    ELSE:
                      set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join unescape_str:ec)
    ELSE:
      set unescape_str:seg to (unescape_str:seg join unescape_str:c)
    change unescape_str:si by 1
  set unescape_str:return to unescape_str:seg

DEFINE FAST read_number:
  set read_number:s to ""
  set read_number:has_dot to 0
  is_eof c: ch
  set @560 to is_eof:return
  is_digit c: ch
  set @561 to is_digit:return
  ceq a: ch b: "."
  set @562 to ceq:return
  REPEAT UNTIL (@560 = 1) or (not ((@561 = 1) or ((@562 = 1) and (not (read_number:has_dot = 1))))):
    ceq a: ch b: "."
    set @563 to ceq:return
    IF @563 = 1 THEN:
      set read_number:has_dot to 1
    set read_number:s to (read_number:s join ch)
    advance
    is_eof c: ch
    set @560 to is_eof:return
    is_digit c: ch
    set @561 to is_digit:return
    ceq a: ch b: "."
    set @562 to ceq:return
  ceq a: ch b: "e"
  set @564 to ceq:return
  ceq a: ch b: "E"
  set @565 to ceq:return
  IF (@564 = 1) or (@565 = 1) THEN:
    set read_number:has_dot to 1
    set read_number:s to (read_number:s join ch)
    advance
    ceq a: ch b: "+"
    set @566 to ceq:return
    ceq a: ch b: "-"
    set @567 to ceq:return
    IF (@566 = 1) or (@567 = 1) THEN:
      set read_number:s to (read_number:s join ch)
      advance
    is_eof c: ch
    set @568 to is_eof:return
    is_digit c: ch
    set @569 to is_digit:return
    REPEAT UNTIL (@568 = 1) or (not (@569 = 1)):
      set read_number:s to (read_number:s join ch)
      advance
      is_eof c: ch
      set @568 to is_eof:return
      is_digit c: ch
      set @569 to is_digit:return
  set read_number:return to read_number:s

DEFINE FAST eval_re name: (name) args: (args):

    str_eq s1: (name) s2: "re.match"

DEFINE FAST m_fact n: (n):
  vnum v: (n)
  set @665 to vnum:return
  set m_fact:v to (floor of @665)
  set m_fact:r to 1
  set m_fact:i to 2
  REPEAT UNTIL m_fact:i > m_fact:v:
    set m_fact:r to (m_fact:r * m_fact:i)
    change m_fact:i by 1
  mk_num n: m_fact:r
  set @2891 to mk_num:return
  set m_fact:return to @2891

DEFINE FAST s_isdigit s: (s):
  set s_isdigit:r to 1
  vstr v: (s)
  set @666 to vstr:return
  set s_isdigit:ss to @666
  set s_isdigit:i to 1
  set s_isdigit:l to (length of s_isdigit:ss)
  IF s_isdigit:l = 0 THEN:
    set s_isdigit:return to 0
    stop this script
  REPEAT s_isdigit:l:
    is_digit c: (letter s_isdigit:i of s_isdigit:ss)
    set @667 to is_digit:return
    IF not (@667 = 1) THEN:
      set s_isdigit:r to 0
    change s_isdigit:i by 1
  set s_isdigit:return to s_isdigit:r

DEFINE FAST read_ident:
  set read_ident:s to ""
  is_eof c: ch
  set @668 to is_eof:return
  is_alnum c: ch
  set @669 to is_alnum:return
  REPEAT UNTIL (@668 = 1) or (not (@669 = 1)):
    set read_ident:s to (read_ident:s join ch)
    advance
    is_eof c: ch
    set @668 to is_eof:return
    is_alnum c: ch
    set @669 to is_alnum:return
  set read_ident:return to read_ident:s

DEFINE FAST parse_compare:
  parse_bitor
  set @670 to parse_bitor:return
  set parse_compare:l to @670
  IF err = 1 THEN:
    mk_num n: 0
    set @2893 to mk_num:return
    set parse_compare:return to @2893
    stop this script
  IF ((((((((tok = "==") or (tok = "!=")) or (("z" join tok) = "z<")) or (("z" join tok) = "z>")) or (tok = "<=")) or (tok = ">=")) or (tok = "is")) or (tok = "in")) or (tok = "not") THEN:
    new_list
    set @671 to new_list:return
    set parse_compare:ops to @671
    new_list
    set @672 to new_list:return
    set parse_compare:exprs to @672
    list_append lid2: parse_compare:exprs val: parse_compare:l
    set parse_compare:done to 0
    REPEAT UNTIL parse_compare:done = 1:
      set parse_compare:op to tok
      IF tok = "is" THEN:
        next_tok
        set @673 to next_tok:return
        set _d to @673
        IF err = 1 THEN:
          mk_num n: 0
          set @2894 to mk_num:return
          set parse_compare:return to @2894
          stop this script
        IF tok = "not" THEN:
          set parse_compare:op to "is not"
          next_tok
          set @674 to next_tok:return
          set _d to @674
          IF err = 1 THEN:
            mk_num n: 0
            set @2895 to mk_num:return
            set parse_compare:return to @2895
            stop this script
        ELSE:
          set parse_compare:op to "is"
      ELSE:
        IF tok = "not" THEN:
          next_tok
          set @675 to next_tok:return
          set _d to @675
          IF err = 1 THEN:
            mk_num n: 0
            set @2896 to mk_num:return
            set parse_compare:return to @2896
            stop this script
          IF tok = "in" THEN:
            set parse_compare:op to "not in"
            next_tok
            set @676 to next_tok:return
            set _d to @676
            IF err = 1 THEN:
              mk_num n: 0
              set @2897 to mk_num:return
              set parse_compare:return to @2897
              stop this script
          ELSE:
            set err to 1
            set errmsg to "SyntaxError: invalid syntax"
            mk_num n: 0
            set @2898 to mk_num:return
            set parse_compare:return to @2898
            stop this script
        ELSE:
          next_tok
          set @677 to next_tok:return
          set _d to @677
          IF err = 1 THEN:
            mk_num n: 0
            set @2899 to mk_num:return
            set parse_compare:return to @2899
            stop this script
      IF (not (parse_compare:done = 1)) and (not (parse_compare:op = "")) THEN:
        mk_str s: parse_compare:op
        set @678 to mk_str:return
        list_append lid2: parse_compare:ops val: @678
        _push v: parse_compare:l
        _push v: parse_compare:done
        _push v: parse_compare:op
        _push v: parse_compare:ops
        _push v: parse_compare:exprs
        parse_bitor
        set @679 to parse_bitor:return
        set parse_compare:r to @679
        _pop
        set @680 to _pop:return
        set parse_compare:exprs to @680
        _pop
        set @681 to _pop:return
        set parse_compare:ops to @681
        _pop
        set @682 to _pop:return
        set parse_compare:op to @682
        _pop
        set @683 to _pop:return
        set parse_compare:done to @683
        _pop
        set @684 to _pop:return
        set parse_compare:l to @684
        IF err = 1 THEN:

DEFINE FAST s_replace s: (s) old: (old) new: (new):
  vstr v: (s)
  set @1536 to vstr:return
  set s_replace:ss to @1536
  vstr v: (old)
  set @1537 to vstr:return
  set s_replace:oo to @1537
  vstr v: (new)
  set @1538 to vstr:return
  set s_replace:nn to @1538
  set s_replace:r to ""
  set s_replace:i to 1
  set s_replace:sl to (length of s_replace:ss)
  set s_replace:ol to (length of s_replace:oo)
  IF s_replace:ol = 0 THEN:
    mk_str s: s_replace:ss
    set @3243 to mk_str:return
    set s_replace:return to @3243
    stop this script
  REPEAT UNTIL s_replace:i > s_replace:sl:
    s_sub s: s_replace:ss start: s_replace:i end: (s_replace:i + s_replace:ol)
    set @3244 to s_sub:return
    str_eq s1: @3244 s2: s_replace:oo
    set @1539 to str_eq:return
    IF (not (((s_replace:i + s_replace:ol) - 1) > s_replace:sl)) and (@1539 = 1) THEN:

DEFINE FAST m_pow_mod b: (b) e: (e) m: (m):
  vnum v: (b)
  set @1546 to vnum:return
  set m_pow_mod:base to (floor of @1546)
  vnum v: (e)
  set @1547 to vnum:return
  set m_pow_mod:exp to (floor of @1547)
  vnum v: (m)
  set @1548 to vnum:return
  set m_pow_mod:modu to (floor of @1548)
  IF m_pow_mod:modu = 1 THEN:
    mk_num n: 0
    set @3257 to mk_num:return
    set m_pow_mod:return to @3257
    stop this script
  IF m_pow_mod:exp < 0 THEN:
    set err to 1
    set errmsg to "ValueError: pow() 2nd argument cannot be negative when 3rd argument specified"
    mk_none
    set @3258 to mk_none:return
    set m_pow_mod:return to @3258
    stop this script
  set m_pow_mod:result to 1
  set m_pow_mod:base to (m_pow_mod:base mod m_pow_mod:modu)
  REPEAT UNTIL m_pow_mod:exp = 0:
    IF (m_pow_mod:exp mod 2) = 1 THEN:
      set m_pow_mod:result to ((m_pow_mod:result * m_pow_mod:base) mod m_pow_mod:modu)
    set m_pow_mod:exp to (floor of (m_pow_mod:exp / 2))
    set m_pow_mod:base to ((m_pow_mod:base * m_pow_mod:base) mod m_pow_mod:modu)
  mk_num n: m_pow_mod:result
  set @3259 to mk_num:return
  set m_pow_mod:return to @3259

DEFINE FAST esc_str s: (s):
  set esc_str:r to ""
  set esc_str:i to 1
  set esc_str:l to (length of (s))
  REPEAT esc_str:l:
    set esc_str:c to (letter esc_str:i of (s))
    is_nl c: esc_str:c
    set @1829 to is_nl:return
    IF @1829 = 1 THEN:
      set esc_str:r to (esc_str:r join "\n")
    ELSE:
      ceq a: esc_str:c b: "	"
      set @1830 to ceq:return
      IF @1830 = 1 THEN:
        set esc_str:r to (esc_str:r join "\t")
      ELSE:
        ceq a: esc_str:c b: "\"
        set @1831 to ceq:return
        IF @1831 = 1 THEN:
          set esc_str:r to (esc_str:r join "\\")
        ELSE:
          ceq a: esc_str:c b: "'"
          set @1832 to ceq:return
          IF @1832 = 1 THEN:
            set esc_str:r to (esc_str:r join "\'")
          ELSE:
            ceq a: esc_str:c b: """
            set @1833 to ceq:return
            IF @1833 = 1 THEN:
              set esc_str:r to (esc_str:r join "\"")
            ELSE:
              ceq a: esc_str:c b: ""
              set @1834 to ceq:return
              IF @1834 = 1 THEN:
                set esc_str:r to (esc_str:r join "\r")
              ELSE:
                set esc_str:r to (esc_str:r join esc_str:c)
    change esc_str:i by 1
  set esc_str:return to esc_str:r

DEFINE FAST s_isspace s: (s):
  set s_isspace:r to 1
  vstr v: (s)
  set @1904 to vstr:return
  set s_isspace:ss to @1904
  set s_isspace:i to 1
  set s_isspace:l to (length of s_isspace:ss)
  IF s_isspace:l = 0 THEN:
    set s_isspace:return to 0
    stop this script
  REPEAT s_isspace:l:
    is_ws c: (letter s_isspace:i of s_isspace:ss)
    set @1905 to is_ws:return
    IF not (@1905 = 1) THEN:
      set s_isspace:r to 0
    change s_isspace:i by 1
  set s_isspace:return to s_isspace:r

DEFINE FAST list_pop lid2: (lid2) i: (i):
  set list_pop:sz to (item (lid2) of l_count)
  IF list_pop:sz = 0 THEN:
    set err to 1
    set errmsg to "IndexError: pop from empty list"
    mk_none
    set @3475 to mk_none:return
    set list_pop:return to @3475
    stop this script
  set list_pop:pi to (i)
  IF list_pop:pi < -99999 THEN:
    set list_pop:pi to (list_pop:sz - 1)
  IF list_pop:pi < 0 THEN:
    set list_pop:pi to (list_pop:sz + list_pop:pi)
  IF (list_pop:pi < 0) or (not (list_pop:pi < list_pop:sz)) THEN:
    set err to 1
    set errmsg to "IndexError: pop index out of range"
    mk_none
    set @3476 to mk_none:return
    set list_pop:return to @3476
    stop this script
  list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_pop:pi
  set @1906 to list_get:return
  set list_pop:val to @1906
  set list_pop:ns to li
  set list_pop:j to 0
  REPEAT list_pop:sz:
    IF not (list_pop:j = list_pop:pi) THEN:
      list_get lid2: (lid2) i: list_pop:j
      set @1907 to list_get:return
      set list_pop:item to @1907
      add list_pop:item to l_items
      change li by 1
    change list_pop:j by 1
  replace item (lid2) of l_start with list_pop:ns
  replace item (lid2) of l_count with (list_pop:sz - 1)
  set list_pop:return to list_pop:val

DEFINE FAST s_format fmt: (fmt) args: (args) kwargs: (kwargs):

  set s_format:has_name to 0

        set s_format:field to ""

          set s_format:field to (s_format:field join (letter s_format:i of s_format:s))

          change s_format:i by 1

        set json_escape_str:r to (json_escape_str:r join "\"")

            ELSE:

  set json_escape_str:return to json_escape_str:r

DEFINE FAST s_rfind s: (s) sub: (sub) start: (start):
  vstr v: (s)
  set @2164 to vstr:return
  set s_rfind:ss to @2164
  vstr v: (sub)
  set @2165 to vstr:return
  set s_rfind:pp to @2165
  vnum v: (start)
  set @2166 to vnum:return
  set s_rfind:st to @2166
  set s_rfind:sl to (length of s_rfind:ss)
  set s_rfind:pl to (length of s_rfind:pp)
  IF s_rfind:st < 0 THEN:
    set s_rfind:st to (s_rfind:sl + s_rfind:st)
  IF s_rfind:st < 0 THEN:
    set s_rfind:st to 0
  IF s_rfind:pl = 0 THEN:
    mk_num n: s_rfind:sl
    set @3592 to mk_num:return
    set s_rfind:return to @3592
    stop this script
  set s_rfind:i to ((s_rfind:sl - s_rfind:pl) + 1)
  REPEAT UNTIL s_rfind:i < (s_rfind:st + 1):
    set s_rfind:m to 1
    set s_rfind:j to 0
    REPEAT s_rfind:pl:
      ord_c c: (letter (s_rfind:i + s_rfind:j) of s_rfind:ss)
      set @2167 to ord_c:return
      ord_c c: (letter (s_rfind:j + 1) of s_rfind:pp)
      set @2168 to ord_c:return
      IF not (@2167 = @2168) THEN:
        set s_rfind:m to 0
      change s_rfind:j by 1
    IF s_rfind:m = 1 THEN:
      mk_num n: (s_rfind:i - 1)
      set @3593 to mk_num:return
      set s_rfind:return to @3593
      stop this script
    change s_rfind:i by -1
  mk_num n: -1
  set @3594 to mk_num:return
  set s_rfind:return to @3594

DEFINE FAST peekc:

DEFINE FAST list_get lid2: (lid2) i: (i):
  set list_get:sz to (item (lid2) of l_count)
  set list_get:i2 to (i)
  IF list_get:i2 < 0 THEN:
    set list_get:i2 to (list_get:sz + list_get:i2)
  IF (list_get:i2 < 0) or (not (list_get:i2 < list_get:sz)) THEN:
    set err to 1
    set errmsg to "IndexError: list index out of range"
    mk_none
    set @3602 to mk_none:return
    set list_get:return to @3602
    stop this script
  set list_get:return to (item ((item (lid2) of l_start) + list_get:i2) of l_items)

DEFINE FAST s_count s: (s) sub: (sub) start: (start):
  vstr v: (s)
  set @2261 to vstr:return
  set s_count:ss to @2261
  vstr v: (sub)
  set @2262 to vstr:return
  set s_count:pp to @2262
  vnum v: (start)
  set @2263 to vnum:return
  set s_count:st to @2263
  set s_count:sl to (length of s_count:ss)
  set s_count:pl to (length of s_count:pp)
  IF s_count:pl = 0 THEN:
    mk_num n: (s_count:sl + 1)
    set @3637 to mk_num:return
    set s_count:return to @3637
    stop this script
  set s_count:cnt to 0
  set s_count:i to (s_count:st + 1)

    set s_count:m to 1
    set s_count:j to 0
    REPEAT s_count:pl:
      ord_c c: (letter (s_count:i + s_count:j) of s_count:ss)
      set @2264 to ord_c:return
      ord_c c: (letter (s_count:j + 1) of s_count:pp)
      set @2265 to ord_c:return
      IF not (@2264 = @2265) THEN:
        set s_count:m to 0
      change s_count:j by 1
    IF s_count:m = 1 THEN:
      change s_count:cnt by 1
      change s_count:i by s_count:pl
    ELSE:
      change s_count:i by 1
  mk_num n: s_count:cnt
  set @3638 to mk_num:return
  set s_count:return to @3638

DEFINE FAST parse_block:
  expect t: "NEWLINE"
  set @2303 to expect:return
  set _d to @2303
  IF err = 1 THEN:
    mk_num n: 0
    set @3645 to mk_num:return
    set parse_block:return to @3645
    stop this script
  expect t: "INDENT"
  set @2304 to expect:return
  set _d to @2304
  IF err = 1 THEN:
    mk_num n: 0
    set @3646 to mk_num:return
    set parse_block:return to @3646
    stop this script
  new_list
  set @2305 to new_list:return
  set parse_block:stmts to @2305
  set parse_block:bdone to 0
  REPEAT UNTIL ((tok = "DEDENT") or (err = 1)) or (parse_block:bdone = 1):
    IF tok = "NEWLINE" THEN:
      next_tok
      set @2306 to next_tok:return
      set _d to @2306
    IF tok = "DEDENT" THEN:
      set parse_block:bdone to 1
    IF not (parse_block:bdone = 1) THEN:
      _push v: parse_block:stmts
      _push v: parse_block:bdone
      parse_stmt
      set @2307 to parse_stmt:return
      set parse_block:s to @2307
      IF err = 1 THEN:
        mk_num n: 0
        set @3647 to mk_num:return
        set parse_block:return to @3647
        stop this script
      _pop
      set @2308 to _pop:return
      set parse_block:bdone to @2308
      _pop
      set @2309 to _pop:return
      set parse_block:stmts to @2309
      list_append lid2: parse_block:stmts val: parse_block:s
  IF err = 1 THEN:
    mk_num n: 0
    set @3648 to mk_num:return
    set parse_block:return to @3648
    stop this script
  IF tok = "DEDENT" THEN:
    next_tok
    set @2310 to next_tok:return
    set _d to @2310

  set @2311 to new_node:return

DEFINE FAST handle_indent:
  IF paren_depth > 0 THEN:
    is_eof c: ch
    set @2313 to is_eof:return
    is_space c: ch
    set @2314 to is_space:return
    REPEAT UNTIL (@2313 = 1) or (not (@2314 = 1)):
      advance
      is_eof c: ch
      set @2313 to is_eof:return
      is_space c: ch
      set @2314 to is_space:return
    ceq a: ch b: "#"
    set @2315 to ceq:return
    IF @2315 = 1 THEN:
      is_eof c: ch
      set @2316 to is_eof:return
      is_nl c: ch
      set @2317 to is_nl:return
      REPEAT UNTIL (@2316 = 1) or (@2317 = 1):
        advance
        is_eof c: ch
        set @2316 to is_eof:return
        is_nl c: ch
        set @2317 to is_nl:return
    is_nl c: ch
    set @2318 to is_nl:return
    IF @2318 = 1 THEN:
      advance
      handle_indent
      set @3650 to handle_indent:return
      set handle_indent:return to @3650
      stop this script
    set at_line_start to 0
    next_tok
    set @3651 to next_tok:return
    set handle_indent:return to @3651
    stop this script
  set handle_indent:saved_line to line
  set handle_indent:saved_col to col
  set handle_indent:indent to 0
  ceq a: ch b: " "
  set @2319 to ceq:return
  ceq a: ch b: "	"
  set @2320 to ceq:return
  REPEAT UNTIL not ((@2319 = 1) or (@2320 = 1)):
    ceq a: ch b: " "
    set @2321 to ceq:return
    IF @2321 = 1 THEN:
      change handle_indent:indent by 1
    ELSE:
      change handle_indent:indent by 4
    advance
    ceq a: ch b: " "
    set @2319 to ceq:return
    ceq a: ch b: "	"
    set @2320 to ceq:return
  is_eof c: ch
  set @2322 to is_eof:return
  is_nl c: ch
  set @2323 to is_nl:return
  ceq a: ch b: "#"
  set @2324 to ceq:return
  IF ((@2322 = 1) or (@2323 = 1)) or (@2324 = 1) THEN:
    set at_line_start to 0
    skip_ws_cmt
    is_nl c: ch
    set @2325 to is_nl:return
    IF @2325 = 1 THEN:
      advance
      handle_indent
      set @3652 to handle_indent:return
      set handle_indent:return to @3652
      stop this script
    is_eof c: ch
    set @2326 to is_eof:return
    IF @2326 = 1 THEN:
      REPEAT UNTIL not ((length of indent_stack) > 1):
        delete (length of indent_stack) of indent_stack
        set_tok t: "DEDENT" v: ""
        set handle_indent:return to "DEDENT"
        stop this script
      set_tok t: "EOF" v: ""
      set handle_indent:return to "EOF"
      stop this script
  set at_line_start to 0
  set handle_indent:cur_indent to (item (length of indent_stack) of indent_stack)
  IF handle_indent:indent > handle_indent:cur_indent THEN:
    add handle_indent:indent to indent_stack
    set_tok t: "INDENT" v: ""
    set handle_indent:return to "INDENT"
    stop this script
  IF handle_indent:indent < handle_indent:cur_indent THEN:
    set handle_indent:ndedent to 0
    REPEAT UNTIL not ((length of indent_stack) > 1):
      delete (length of indent_stack) of indent_stack
      change handle_indent:ndedent by 1
      set handle_indent:pi to (item (length of indent_stack) of indent_stack)
      IF handle_indent:indent = handle_indent:pi THEN:
        IF handle_indent:ndedent > 1 THEN:
          set pending_dedent_count to (handle_indent:ndedent - 1)
        set_tok t: "DEDENT" v: ""
        set handle_indent:return to "DEDENT"
        stop this script
      IF handle_indent:indent > handle_indent:pi THEN:
        set err to 1
        set errmsg to "IndentationError: unindent does not match any outer indentation level"
        set_tok t: "ERROR" v: ""
        set handle_indent:return to "ERROR"
        stop this script
    set err to 1
    set errmsg to "IndentationError: unexpected unindent"
    set_tok t: "ERROR" v: ""
    set handle_indent:return to "ERROR"
    stop this script
  next_tok
  set @3653 to next_tok:return
  set handle_indent:return to @3653

DEFINE FAST bitwise_xor a: (a) b: (b):
  vnum v: (a)
  set @2327 to vnum:return
  set bitwise_xor:x to (floor of @2327)
  vnum v: (b)
  set @2328 to vnum:return
  set bitwise_xor:y to (floor of @2328)
  set bitwise_xor:r to 0
  set bitwise_xor:bit to 1
  REPEAT UNTIL (bitwise_xor:x = 0) and (bitwise_xor:y = 0):
    IF not ((operator_equals) = (operator_equals)) THEN:
      change bitwise_xor:r by bitwise_xor:bit
    set bitwise_xor:x to (floor of (bitwise_xor:x / 2))
    set bitwise_xor:y to (floor of (bitwise_xor:y / 2))
    set bitwise_xor:bit to (bitwise_xor:bit * 2)
  mk_num n: bitwise_xor:r
  set @3654 to mk_num:return
  set bitwise_xor:return to @3654

DEFINE FAST read_string_raw is_raw: (is_raw):

  advance
  set read_string_raw:triple to 0
  ceq a: ch b: read_string_raw:quote
  set @2460 to ceq:return
  peekc
  set @3708 to peekc:return
  ceq a: @3708 b: read_string_raw:quote
  set @2461 to ceq:return

    advance
    advance
    set read_string_raw:triple to 1
  set read_string_raw:s to ""
  IF read_string_raw:triple = 1 THEN:
    is_eof c: ch
    set @2462 to is_eof:return
    REPEAT UNTIL @2462 = 1:
      ceq a: ch b: read_string_raw:quote
      set @2463 to ceq:return
      IF (@2463 = 1) and (not ((pos + 2) > srclen)) THEN:
        ceq a: (letter (pos + 1) of src) b: read_string_raw:quote

DEFINE FAST re_simple_match pat: (pat) txt: (txt) pos: (pos):
  set re_simple_match:pi to 1
  set re_simple_match:ti to (pos)
  set re_simple_match:pl to (length of (pat))
  set re_simple_match:tl to (length of (txt))
  set re_simple_match:matched to 1
  REPEAT UNTIL (re_simple_match:pi > re_simple_match:pl) or (re_simple_match:matched = 0):
    IF re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl THEN:
      set re_simple_match:matched to 0
    ELSE:
      set re_simple_match:pc to (letter re_simple_match:pi of (pat))
      IF ("z" join re_simple_match:pc) = "z." THEN:
        set re_simple_match:ti to (re_simple_match:ti + 1)
        set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
      ELSE:
        IF ("z" join re_simple_match:pc) = "z*" THEN:
          set re_simple_match:prev to (letter (re_simple_match:pi - 1) of (pat))
          set re_simple_match:nextc to ""
          IF re_simple_match:pi < re_simple_match:pl THEN:
            set re_simple_match:nextc to (letter (re_simple_match:pi + 1) of (pat))
          set re_simple_match:star_done to 0
          REPEAT UNTIL (re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl) or (re_simple_match:star_done = 1):
            IF (not (re_simple_match:nextc = "")) and (not (re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl)) THEN:
              IF (letter re_simple_match:ti of (txt)) = re_simple_match:nextc THEN:
                set re_simple_match:star_done to 1
            IF re_simple_match:star_done = 0 THEN:
              IF not (("z" join re_simple_match:prev) = "z.") THEN:
                IF re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl THEN:
                  set re_simple_match:star_done to 1
                ELSE:
                  IF not ((letter re_simple_match:ti of (txt)) = re_simple_match:prev) THEN:
                    set re_simple_match:star_done to 1
            IF re_simple_match:star_done = 0 THEN:
              set re_simple_match:ti to (re_simple_match:ti + 1)
          set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
        ELSE:
          IF ("z" join re_simple_match:pc) = "z+" THEN:
            set re_simple_match:prev to (letter (re_simple_match:pi - 1) of (pat))
            IF re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl THEN:
              set re_simple_match:matched to 0
            ELSE:
              IF (not (("z" join re_simple_match:prev) = "z.")) and (not ((letter re_simple_match:ti of (txt)) = re_simple_match:prev)) THEN:
                set re_simple_match:matched to 0
            IF re_simple_match:matched = 1 THEN:
              set re_simple_match:ti to (re_simple_match:ti + 1)
              set re_simple_match:plus_done to 0
              REPEAT UNTIL (re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl) or (re_simple_match:plus_done = 1):
                IF (not (("z" join re_simple_match:prev) = "z.")) and (not (re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl)) THEN:
                  IF not ((letter re_simple_match:ti of (txt)) = re_simple_match:prev) THEN:
                    set re_simple_match:plus_done to 1
                IF re_simple_match:plus_done = 0 THEN:
                  set re_simple_match:ti to (re_simple_match:ti + 1)
              set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
          ELSE:
            IF ("z" join re_simple_match:pc) = "z?" THEN:
              set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
            ELSE:
              IF (("z" join re_simple_match:pc) = "z\") and (re_simple_match:pi < re_simple_match:pl) THEN:
                set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
                set re_simple_match:pc to (letter re_simple_match:pi of (pat))
                IF re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl THEN:
                  set re_simple_match:matched to 0
                ELSE:
                  IF not ((letter re_simple_match:ti of (txt)) = re_simple_match:pc) THEN:
                    set re_simple_match:matched to 0
                  ELSE:
                    set re_simple_match:ti to (re_simple_match:ti + 1)
                    set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
              ELSE:
                IF ("z" join re_simple_match:pc) = "z^" THEN:
                  IF not (re_simple_match:ti = 1) THEN:
                    set re_simple_match:matched to 0
                  ELSE:
                    set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
                ELSE:
                  IF ("z" join re_simple_match:pc) = "z$" THEN:
                    IF not (re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl) THEN:
                      set re_simple_match:matched to 0
                    ELSE:
                      set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
                  ELSE:
                    IF re_simple_match:ti > re_simple_match:tl THEN:
                      set re_simple_match:matched to 0
                    ELSE:
                      IF not ((letter re_simple_match:ti of (txt)) = re_simple_match:pc) THEN:
                        set re_simple_match:matched to 0
                      ELSE:
                        set re_simple_match:ti to (re_simple_match:ti + 1)
                        set re_simple_match:pi to (re_simple_match:pi + 1)
  IF (re_simple_match:matched = 1) and (not ((re_simple_match:ti - (pos)) < 0)) THEN:
    set re_simple_match:return to (re_simple_match:ti - (pos))
    stop this script
  set re_simple_match:return to 0

  set @2642 to vdata:return
  set inst_class:d to @2642
  set inst_class:p to 1
  set inst_class:l to (length of inst_class:d)
  REPEAT UNTIL inst_class:p > inst_class:l:
    IF ("z" join (letter inst_class:p of inst_class:d)) = "z:" THEN:
      s_sub s: inst_class:d start: 1 end: inst_class:p
      set @3815 to s_sub:return
      set inst_class:return to @3815
      stop this script
    change inst_class:p by 1
  set inst_class:return to 0

DEFINE FAST parse_not_test:
  IF tok = "not" THEN:
    next_tok
    set @2643 to next_tok:return
    set _d to @2643
    IF err = 1 THEN:
      mk_num n: 0
      set @3816 to mk_num:return
      set parse_not_test:return to @3816
      stop this script
    parse_not_test
    set @2644 to parse_not_test:return
    set parse_not_test:f to @2644
    IF err = 1 THEN:
      mk_num n: 0
      set @3817 to mk_num:return
      set parse_not_test:return to @3817
      stop this script
    mk_str s: "not"
    set @3989 to mk_str:return
    mk_node2 nt: "unaryop" a: @3989 b: parse_not_test:f
    set @3818 to mk_node2:return
    set parse_not_test:return to @3818
    stop this script
  parse_compare
  set @3819 to parse_compare:return
  set parse_not_test:return to @3819

WHEN I receive "RunTheInterpreter":
  run_the_interpreter

WHEN I receive "displayClean":
  stop other scripts in sprite
